# 1. Get mode and scope_key\n                mode = exec_context.mode  # \"project\" or \"sentinel\"\n                if mode == \"sentinel\":\n                    scope_key = exec_context.sentinel_day  # e.g., \"2026-01-03\"\n                else:\n                    scope_key = exec_context.execution_id  # UUID\n\n                # 3. Hash it (full SHA-256, no truncation)\n                identity_hash = hashlib.sha256(identity.encode()).hexdigest()\n\n                return identity_hash, {\n                    \"repo_root\": repo_root,\n                    \"mode\": mode,\n                    \"scope_key\": scope_key,\n                    \"agent_key\": agent_key,\n                }\n\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/server.py", "byte_size": 26996, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 4, "line_count": 619, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "server.py", "sha256": "b5ea3b09b035866838266679c960fcec0dfe4ed1e244e55d768ffe002a7b5a92"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T09:40:36.536355Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
